# Forward audit — M6.P2 T23, the generic packages no image builds (#670)

**The read:** the 1,604 rows at `8ebaf8daad` (unchanged at the dispatch tip `27b015c901`). **Depth:** Local, no reviewer seat; the counts are the check. **The rule (D-WORKFLOW-209):** nothing under `packages/`, `projects/` or `tools/` changes on this branch. Every figure cites `stage-a/` or `stage-b/`. The tables are generated by `tools/t23-tables.py`, which asserts 1,604 files, 727 recipes and 731 units.

**Can this be done on the VM? Yes -- on the host.** The inputs are the inventory's rows, five generated build plans (`tools/viewplan`: plan generation, about a second each), `get_pkg_directory`, `git grep` and `pkgcheck`. Nothing needs a guest, a device, a compiler or a provider. **The network:**
- The first five plan runs (04:12:13 to 04:12:16 UTC) each made one HTTPS request, through `mesa-reusable:15`'s global-scope `curl` (§ The runs). This is recorded as a deviation.
- Every later plan run went through a `curl` shim, and no other network was used.

## The split

| Quantity | The coordinator (host plans) | The lane, host plans | The lane, host and container plans | Command |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | --- |
| recipes in the set whose name some plan builds | 84 | 84 | **86** | `tools/t23-tables.py <tree> stats` |
| ... of them shadowed copies (the name resolves to another directory) | 84 | 84 | 86 | the same |
| shadowed units with no plan naming the name | 5 | 5 (`swig`, `amlogic-boot-fip`, `atf`, `rkbin`, `weston`) | **3** (`amlogic-boot-fip`, `rkbin`, `weston`) | the same |
| ... of them with the override dead too | 3 | -- | 3 | the same |

**The delta, stated:** `swig` and `atf` are H700's bootloader chain, which the inventory's container plan builds (`reachable_by` names H700-aarch64) and the host plan cannot, because `xmlstarlet` is absent here (§ The runs). The table uses the host and container plans together, so both are *shadowed copy, override live*.

**The eleven basename pairs**, resolved with `get_pkg_directory` on GENERIC_X64, H700 and SM8550 (each pair resolves to the `projects/ROCKNIX` copy on all three):

## Reachability

One row per unit (731).
- **Files, lines:** of the unit's rows in the set.
- **Tier:** fork-own when any row is.
- **Inventory reach:** the recipe row's, or for the four units whose recipe is outside the set, the files'.
- **`shadowed_by`:** the inventory's.
- **Named by a plan:** the devices whose host or container plan builds the name.
- **Resolves to:** `get_pkg_directory <name>` on each device.
- **Unpacked by:** a plan-built recipe whose `PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK` names it, with its line, through the closure.
- **Disposition:** one of brief § 1's six.

## Disagreements

Every unit where the inventory's `reach` and the lane's plans differ. **Five units, all one cause:** `tools/renovation-inventory`'s `compute_reach` follows the plans and `. ${ROOT}/packages/...` sourcing, not `PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK`.

| Unit | The inventory says | The lane's evidence | The cause |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/graphics/spirv-headers` (fork-own) | `reach: unreached` | unpacked on all three devices by the overrides `glslang:18`, `shaderc:12`, `spirv-tools:18` and `llvm:17`, all four in all three plans | `PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK` not followed |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/rust/cargo-snapshot` | `reach: unreached` | unpacked by the overrides `rust:11` and `cargo:9`, both plan-built | the same |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/rust/rustc-snapshot` | `reach: unreached` | unpacked by the override `rust:11` | the same |
| `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/rust/rust-std-snapshot` | `reach: unreached` | unpacked by the override `rust:11` | the same |
| `packages/devel/binutils-gold` | `reach: unreached` | unpacked by the generic `binutils:11`, in all three plans | the same |

**Not disagreements, and why:**
- **The four units whose recipe is outside the set:** each recipe is sourced at line 4 by a ROCKNIX override (`graphics/gtk3`, `sysutils/btrfs-progs`, `security/openssl`, `sysutils/udevil`), which carries its own copies of the files the recipe reads through `${PKG_DIR}`. The generic directories' other files are unused, so the inventory's `unreached` on them is right.
- **H700's six bootloader names** separate the lane's host plan from the inventory's container plan, not the lane from the inventory. The table follows the container where the host cannot see (§ The runs).

The `bug` on `tools/renovation-inventory` (stage D) carries all five, with a constructed case: a recipe reached only through `PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK` reads `unreached`. The three rust snapshots are among the eight rows upstream deleted after the base, so the fork still unpacks files upstream no longer carries (D-WORKFLOW-198's class).

## The runs

| Run | Result | A FAIL would mean |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `tools/viewplan image`, GENERIC_X64-x86_64 | rc 0, 1 s; `Packages loaded : 1435`; 593 names; the header's 593 | the recipes could not be resolved into a plan |
| `tools/viewplan image`, H700-aarch64 | rc 0, 1 s; 1438; **605**; the header's **611** | as above. The six missing names are the bootloader chain: `SUBDEVICES` is read through `xmlstarlet ... 2>/dev/null` (`distributions/ROCKNIX/config/functions:224`), and `xmlstarlet` is absent on the host, so the host's `u-boot` wants only `toolchain` |
| `tools/viewplan arm`, H700-arm | rc 0, 1 s; 1438; 235; 235 | as above |
| `tools/viewplan image`, SM8550-aarch64 | rc 0, 0 s; 1435; 672; 672 | as above |
| `tools/viewplan arm`, SM8550-arm | rc 0, 1 s; 1435; 238; 238 | as above |
| the five again, offline through a `curl` shim | rc 0 each; **byte-identical** to the first runs; 5 `curl` calls intercepted | -- (the proof that the first runs reached the network once each and that the plan does not depend on it) |
| the `pkgjson` worker on `packages/graphics/mesa-reusable` with the shim | rc 0; the shim logged `curl --fail --connect-timeout 30 --retry 3 --location --max-redirs 5 https://github.com/LibreELEC/mesa-reusable/releases/download/<version>/<file>` | -- (a recipe's global scope reaches the network whenever any plan is generated; a `bug` at stage D) |
| `get_pkg_directory`, every unit name on GENERIC_X64, H700 and SM8550 (`stage-a/resolve-units.txt`) | 2,160 lines, 0 errors; `busybox`, `go`, `installer`, `spirv-headers`, `cbindgen`, `swig` and `atf` each resolve to their `projects/ROCKNIX` override on all three | a name could not be resolved, or resolves to more than one directory |
| `get_pkg_directory`, the unpack graph's other 50 names | 150 lines; the 15 empty are another device's own package (`u-boot-DDR3` on SM8550) | -- |
| `git grep -E '^\s*PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK\+?=' 8ebaf8daad -- packages projects` | 94 lines (40 indented `=`, 38 anchored `=`) | -- |
| **`tools/pkgcheck` as the brief wrote it** (`tools/pkgcheck packages/addons/tools/jre.zulu`) | **rc 0, no output: nothing checked.** The tool matches `-path */<arg>/package.mk`, and `find packages projects` prints no leading `./` | -- (a `bug` at stage D: an argument that matches no recipe should not exit 0) |
| `tools/pkgcheck <path less its first component>`, over the 727 | **727 processed** (each named in the tool's stderr progress line); 3 recipes exit non-zero; 4 FAIL and 14 WARN, in 55 s | a late-binding or duplicate-function fault in a recipe |
| T6's lead, `packages/addons/tools/jre.zulu/package.mk:12` | `FAIL late binding violation: ref PKG_SHORTDESC`. `PKG_SHORTDESC` is set at `:11` of the same recipe, so **a false positive** | a global-scope read of a name only set later |
| T6's lead, `packages/graphics/mesa-reusable/package.mk:14` | `FAIL late binding violation: ref PKG_SOURCE_NAME`. Set at `:13`, so **a false positive** | the same |
| `packages/audio/wireplumber` | 2 `FAIL duplicate function def` | #657's parser false positive, as T6 recorded |

## Proofs

| Claim | Evidence | proved: |
| --- | --- | --- |
| The 1,604, their counts and the 731 units (727 + 4) | `stage-a/scope.out`; `tools/t23-tables.py` asserts | proved: tree |
| Nothing under `packages/`, `projects/` or `tools/` changed from the dispatch tip, and `packages/`/`projects/` equal `8ebaf8daad` | `git diff --stat` (`00`) | proved: history |
| The five plans' counts beside the header's; H700's six named | `stage-a/plans.out`, `stage-a/plans/` | proved: tool-run |
| The H700 delta is `xmlstarlet`'s absence | `command -v xmlstarlet` empty; `distributions/ROCKNIX/config/functions:224`; the host plan's `u-boot` wanting only `toolchain` | proved: tree |
| Plan generation runs `curl` through `mesa-reusable:15` | the shim's log (`stage-a/shim/`); the offline plans byte-identical | proved: fixture |
| The 84/5 split on host plans; 86/3 with the container | `stage-a/stats.out` | proved: tool-run |
| The eleven pairs resolve to the ROCKNIX copies | `stage-a/resolve-units.txt` | proved: tool-run |
| Five units reached after all through `PKG_DEPENDS_UNPACK` | `stage-a/unpack-lines.txt`, the plans, the resolutions; `stage-a/stats.out` | proved: tool-run |
| The four outside units are shadowed copies, not tool errors | the overrides' line 4 `. ${ROOT}/packages/...`; the overrides' own files present | proved: tree |
| `installer`: shadowed copy, override live | resolves to `projects/ROCKNIX/packages/tools/installer` on all three; in the GENERIC_X64 plan | proved: tool-run |
| `cbindgen` (ROCKNIX) is dead: named by nothing outside its own directory, not on `upstream/next` | `git grep -n cbindgen 8ebaf8daad -- packages projects` (`stage-b/cbindgen-grep.txt`); `git cat-file -e upstream/next:...` fails | proved: tree |
| The brief's `pkgcheck` form checks nothing | `tools/pkgcheck packages/addons/tools/jre.zulu` rc 0, no output; `tools/pkgcheck tools/jre.zulu` rc 1 with the FAIL | proved: tool-run |
| T6's two leads are false positives | the recipes' lines 11-14 at `8ebaf8daad` | proved: tree |
| 11 upstream-carried rows differ from `upstream/next` (8 deleted, 3 modified) | `stage-b/upstream-diff.txt` | proved: history |
| Upstream has made 0 commits on `packages/` since the base | `git log --oneline c445081a59..upstream/next -- packages` | proved: history |
